## 4. WIDGET FAILURES

Expected failures are ScrollTable and HighlightText under svg/png: those widgets
render no SVG, so the export correctly refuses. Anything else is a defect.

Campaign runs are numbered in issue order. A failure whose run range starts in
the hundreds means something accumulates over the campaign; one starting at a low
number means it was broken from the start. Total exports issued: 2157.
